([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna--c895d9e6c25e5a7e))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., AnnData .h5ad) has been QC-filtered and normalized and you need a compact latent representation for clustering or UMAP visualization, such as reproducing HuBMAP spleen cluster counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna--260081bc53a44a5a))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrnaseq**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix (tall or wide format, e.g. CSV/Parquet) must be projected to a low-dimensional space to enable scalable clustering on large cell counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrnaseq))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell**: Use when a sparse single-cell epigenomic count matrix (reads-in-peaks scATAC-seq or scChIP-seq bins) has passed QC filtering and normalization and a low-dimensional embedding is needed for clustering or 2D visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq annotation pipeline has cells rejected as 'unassigned' after marker counting and needs a low-dimensional embedding (PCA) plus clustering to reassign them by cluster similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-unsupervised**: Use when you have a feature-by-sample matrix (rows = annotated chemical features such as m/z, retention time, GNPS spectral library matches; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-unsupervised) · 2 copies)
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when after a differential-expression test on background-subtracted microarray data, you need a low-dimensional (PCA) visualization to confirm that samples cluster by response class using only the significant genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-visualization))
- **pca-dmd-neural-dynamics-reconstruction**: PCA-DMD for neural dynamics reconstruction. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/pca-dmd-neural-dynamics-reconstruction) · ★4)
- **pca-embedding-batch-assessment**: Use when you need to quantify residual batch effects after correction (e.g. ComBat on two-technical-replicate mESC data) in a linear PC space, or to build the PCA baseline needed for a sensitivity comparison against a nonlinear embedding such as BEENE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-embedding-batch-assessment))
- **pca-embedding-of-deviation-scores**: Use when you need a low-dimensional cell embedding from scATAC-seq accessibility counts for clustering and want to compare it against alternatives such as SnapATAC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pca-embedding-of-deviation-scores))
- **pca-embedding-of-trajectory-data**: Use when pseudotime has been inferred with redPATH's consensus Hamiltonian path algorithm and you need to detect and visualize whether the developmental trajectory is linear or branched. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-embedding-of-trajectory-data))
- **pca-embedding-visualization**: Use when a trained deep metabolomics model (e.g. DenseNet121 on raw LC-MS .npy spectra) must be inspected layer-by-layer to show how hidden representations shed batch information and gain disease-class information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-embedding-visualization))
- **pca-eval**: Evaluates embodied decision-making capabilities across three dimensions: perception (visual understanding), cognition (reasoning and task breakdown), and action (executing correct steps or decisions). It tests models in autonomous driving, domestic assistance, and game-playing environments. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PCA-EVAL, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Action Score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pca-eval) · ★3)
- **pca-feature-representation-extraction**: Use when preparing a cell-typed scRNA-seq .h5ad (AnnData) as input to scValue or another OOB random-forest sketching step that expects a dense PCA feature matrix via use_rep='X_pca'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-feature-representation-extraction))
- **pca-glasso-network-overlay**: Use when high-dimensional longitudinal metabolomics data (e.g., NMR-derived metabolite concentrations across developmental stages) must be decomposed into transition-specific covariance subnetworks linking metabolites to specific cell-type transitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-glasso-network-overlay))
- **pca-gmm-clustering-of-scrnaseq**: Use when a marker-count-based cell type identifier leaves many cells unassigned and you need to reassign them by within-cluster expression similarity, or when reproducing the 0.1-0.9 rejection/reassignment threshold sweep. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-gmm-clustering-of-scrnaseq))
- **pca-hotelling-t2-outlier-detection**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics data table with QCpool samples measured at regular sequence intervals must be screened for outlying samples before downstream analysis, i.e. during the 'removal of outlying samples' step of a multistep QC protocol. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-hotelling-t2-outlier-detection))
- **pca-input-feature-scaling**: Use when a raw metabolite concentration/intensity table (e.g., 90 metabolites from integrated NMR peak areas across hESC→EB→Rosette→hNPC→Neuron stages) must be scaled before PCA so features contribute comparably to components. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-input-feature-scaling))
- **pca-input-formatting-for-trajectory-inference**: Use when preparing PC-reduced single-cell omics matrices (scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq chromVAR k-mer or TF-motif z-scores) as input to VIA's via_wrapper, or when testing trajectory-inference robustness across PC counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-input-formatting-for-trajectory-inference))
- **pca-kmeans-cell-type-initialization**: Use when running DIEM on 10X droplet-based snRNA-seq (or scRNA-seq) count data and you need to initialize k cell-type clusters via PCA and k-means before run_em debris-score estimation and call_targets filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-kmeans-cell-type-initialization))
- **pca-latent-factor-detection**: Use when a scRNA-Seq expression matrix shows within-cell-type heterogeneity suspected to be a cell-cycle effect, and you need to test which principal components are cell-cycle driven before removing them while preserving cell-type signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-latent-factor-detection))
- **pca-latent-space-coordinate-scoring**: Use when you have normalized single-cell feature expression (protein counts, RNA, accessibility) plus a reduced-dimensional latent space (e.g., 50 protein-based PCs stored in an AnnData object) and need clustering-independent prediction of differentially active features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-latent-space-coordinate-scoring))
- **pca-latent-space-embedding**: Use when a library-normalized (scaled, non-log-transformed) genes x cells expression matrix must be embedded into a low-dimensional latent space before building a KNN graph or PhyloVision report, especially when cell counts exceed available memory (VISION 'pool' defaults to 'auto', micropooling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-latent-space-embedding))
- **pca-linear-baseline-ordering**: Use when benchmarking a circular/periodic pseudo-time method against a linear baseline: scRNA-seq count matrices where cells should be ordered along a latent process and you need PCA-derived PC1/PC2 orderings for comparison (e.g. GSEA NES ranking as in Fig. 4a). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-linear-baseline-ordering))
- **pca-linear-embedding-baseline-construction**: Use when you need a standard linear embedding baseline in PC space to benchmark a learned nonlinear embedding's sensitivity for batch effect estimation metrics like kBET or iLISI on single-cell RNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-linear-embedding-baseline-construction))
- **pca-loading-biomarker-prioritization**: Use when an untargeted DI-MS or LC-MS peak table has been normalized in MetaboShiny (filtered, normalized, transformed, scaled) and you need to rank which m/z values separate a disease group from controls before annotating candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loading-biomarker-prioritization))
- **pca-loading-interpretation-for-bmi-variance**: Use when you have simulated or measured 1D 1H NMR spectra of blood mixtures tied to a continuous outcome such as BMI, and need to determine which principal component captures the outcome-related variance and which metabolites drive it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loading-interpretation-for-bmi-variance))
- **pca-loading-nightingale-chart-rendering**: Use when a user has a multivariate dataset (e.g. astronomical catalogs like SDSS supernova host properties, mock galaxy catalogs, or ZENS) and needs a visual read-out of which variables drive each principal component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/pca-loading-nightingale-chart-rendering))
- **pca-loading-versus-embedding-metagene-comparison**: Use when a gene-embedding workflow has identified a candidate metagene (e.g. an IFIT1/ISG15/OAS1 cluster in interferon beta-stimulated PBMCs) and you need to demonstrate whether the program is cell-type-independent or an artifact of linear-model loading structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-loading-versus-embedding-metagene-comparison))
- **pca-loadings-and-explained-variance-interpretation**: Use when a small, feature-rich metabolomics or omics table (samples x metabolites) needs unbiased dimensionality reduction and you must inspect per-component loadings, sample scores, and explained variance to judge which components capture significant variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loadings-and-explained-variance-interpretation))
- **pca-loadings-lipid-interpretation**: Use when mass spectrometry imaging data (imzML) has been extracted, deisotoped, annotated and TIC-normalised, and the goal is to separate spatially co-located tissue regions (e.g. cerebellum layers) or interpret which lipids drive each principal component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loadings-lipid-interpretation))
- **pca-model-parameter-configuration**: Use when building a struct model object in R (e.g. PCA from struct/structToolbox) and you need a typed, documented, scalar-constrained parameter such as number_components, accessible via P$number_components and param_obj(P,'number_components'). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-model-parameter-configuration))
- **pca-multinomial-circadian-time-classification**: Use when you have bulk or pseudobulk transcriptomic data with known circadian timestamps for training and need to predict the circadian time of single, untimestamped samples across assay platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-multinomial-circadian-time-classification))
- **pca-neighborhood-distance-computation**: Use when running scDEED and you need the pre-embedding neighbor distances against which 2D t-SNE/UMAP neighbor distances will be correlated — i.e., after RunPCA on a Seurat object and after generating the Permuted null (row-shuffled scale.data) PCA space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-neighborhood-distance-computation))
- **pca-ordination-procrustes-analysis**: Use when comparing imputed vs original data matrices to quantify distortion of the observation/sample distribution, e.g., benchmarking MNAR imputation methods (GSimp, QRILC, kNN-TN) across graded missingness levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-ordination-procrustes-analysis))
- **pca-outlier-detection-and-missing-value-replacement**: Use when curating or reproducing QC filtering of GCxGC-TOFMS plasma metabolite peak tables — specifically when deciding which detected peaks to exclude (run-order drift, QC deviation, PCA outliers) and which metabolites need missing-value replacement, as in the 691→195 peak filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-outlier-detection-and-missing-value-replacement))
- **pca-outlier-screening**: Use when you have a matrix of NMR-estimated metabolite relative concentrations across plasma samples (e.g. NMRQNet CSV output) and need to detect outlier samples prior to supervised group-difference modeling (OPLS-DA, t-tests). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-outlier-screening))
- **pca-population-structure-analysis**: Use when genome-wide biallelic SNP data (VCF or PLINK BED/BIM/FAM) need population structure inference via PCA and ADMIXTURE, including determining the optimal ancestral components K by CV error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/pca-population-structure-analysis))
- **pca-ppr-generator-xtal**: Motor gerador do Programa de Conservação Auditiva (PCA) e Programa de Proteção Respiratória (PPR) (NR-07 / NR-09). ([clodoaldoperito-droid/ARQUITETURA_JARVIS](https://github.com/clodoaldoperito-droid/ARQUITETURA_JARVIS/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/pca-ppr-generator-xtal) · ★1)
- **pca-preprocessing-of-single-cell-data**: Use when a scRNA-seq expression matrix has thousands of features (e.g. ~23,686 genes) and must be scaled and compressed to PCA-50 prior to training ivis (k=15, model='maaten') for 2D embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-preprocessing-of-single-cell-data))
- **pca-quality-control-of-nmr-spectra**: Use when QC of a large-scale 1H NMR metabonomic dataset (e.g. plasma spectra with pooled QC replicates) is required before metabolome-wide association analysis, to verify analytical reproducibility and absence of batch effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-quality-control-of-nmr-spectra))
- **pca-quality-control-tightness-evaluation**: Use when a corrected metabolomics feature matrix (e.g., post-WaveICA or WaveICA 2.0) must be scored for batch effect removal quality and pooled QCS injections are available to benchmark clustering tightness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-quality-control-tightness-evaluation))
- **pca-reconstruction-error-rejection**: Use when an unlabeled single-cell test set may contain cell populations absent from a labeled training set, and you need to reject (rather than force-classify) cells before hierarchical classification with scHPL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-reconstruction-error-rejection))
- **pca-reserve-analyzer**: Converts a Property Condition Assessment (PCA) into an underwriting-ready capital plan: immediate (Year 0) repair costs, a 10-year replacement-reserve schedule by building component, an implied per-unit-per-year (PUPY) or per-SF reserve, a reserve-adequacy gap versus the lender-underwritten reserve, prioritized diligence issues, and IC questions. Triggers on 'PCA', 'property condition assessment', 'replacement reserve', 'reserve adequacy', 'PNA', or when a property condition report needs to be turned into capital numbers. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/pca-reserve-analyzer) · ★52)
- **pca-score-based-mid-level-fusion**: Use when two or more platform-specific metabolomics datasets (e.g., MS and NMR CSV tables) covering the same samples must be combined into one integrated table and raw column concatenation would duplicate correlated metabolite information across platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-based-mid-level-fusion))
- **pca-score-extraction**: Use when multivariate data in a struct DatasetExperiment needs mean centring followed by PCA, and you must extract principal-component scores (e.g. number_components = 2) as the pipeline output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-extraction))
- **pca-score-interpretation-in-networks**: Use when you have both (1) a sparse network graph from GLASSO showing conditional dependencies among variables, and (2) PCA scores representing variance or loadings in a lower-dimensional space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pca-score-interpretation-in-networks))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization**: Use when a targeted LC–MS preprocessing run (e.g., TARDIS AUC table) needs a global quality check, and you want to confirm QC runs cluster tightly before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization--ef2196d89bfd4869))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization**: Use when a grouped NMR peak feature matrix (samples × peaks) must be checked for class structure: Pareto-scale and mean-center it, run PCA, and inspect the PC1–PC2 score plot colored by wine color for red vs white/rosé separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization--d3f922e29b3e4527))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization**: Use when a LipidomicsExperiment (e.g. imported from Metabolomics Workbench study ST001111 with measure="Area") needs unsupervised quality control, and samples with significantly large dispersion in the PCA scores plot must be identified as outliers before further analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization--bd8df5891cc930c0))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization-of-multi-tissue-data**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics peak-area tables with many zero-count features across multiple tissues and need to check whether samples cluster by study, cohort, genotype, or tissue before downstream stratified analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization-of-multi-tissue-data))
- **pca-scores-interpretation-metabolomics**: Use when you have a peak-value feature matrix from 1D NMR metabolomics spectra and need to visually assess whether experimental groups (e.g., diet concentrations) separate before or alongside differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scores-interpretation-metabolomics))
- **pca-scores-loadings-export**: Use when you have SMolESY-select relative concentration values for the 22 SMolESY-select metabolites and want to verify whether PCA of the panel recapitulates the biomarker signal seen when analysing the full 1H NMR profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scores-loadings-export))
- **pca-scores-significance-testing**: Use when a cleaned, class-labelled X data matrix (DSO with row/column labels and biological/blank class labels) is ready and you need PCA with per-component statistical evidence of class separation rather than visual scores plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scores-significance-testing))
- **pca-scree-biplot-generation**: Use when FT-ICR MS metabolomic samples with assigned molecular formulas need ordination-based group discrimination assessment, complementing PERMANOVA and NMDS, using normalized peak intensities or molecular class composition vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scree-biplot-generation))
- **pca-signal-equivalence-verification**: Use when reconstructing scLENS's RMT noise-filtering module and you must prove that eigen-decomposition of the cell similarity matrix G = XX^T/N yields a signal subspace equivalent to PCA scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-signal-equivalence-verification))
- **pca-skill-manager**: Acelera a criacao e manutencao de skills do Claude. Automatiza o scaffolding de pastas, validacao de metadados YAML e empacotamento ZIP correto. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-agents/pca-skill-manager) · ★67 · 3 copies)
- **pca-test-entry-generator**: 根据最终确认的 Patch Compatibility Analysis findings，生成或细化可运行的测试入口命令、复现程序或项目原生测试代码。在 patch-compatibility-testing 已校验 analysis.json 并生成 test-entry-tasks.json 后使用。 ([neowhy9853/hyl](https://github.com/neowhy9853/hyl/tree/HEAD/%E7%A0%94%E7%A9%B6%E5%86%85%E5%AE%B9%E4%B8%89/patch-compatibility-utils/patch-compatibility-testing/pca-test-entry-generator) · ★2)
- **pca-transcriptional-similarity-bin-assignment**: Use when a binned Visium HD AnnData object carries nucleus/cell labels (from bin2cell.insert_labels and bin2cell.expand_labels) and each 2 μm bin must be assigned to its final cell, with ambiguous equidistant bins resolved by gene-expression similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pca-transcriptional-similarity-bin-assignment))
- **pca-tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when the input is an imzML MSI dataset whose high-dimensional pixel spectra must be reduced to a 2D embedding (PCA→t-SNE) for clustering-based tissue segmentation and hyperparameter comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-tsne-dimensionality-reduction))
- **pca-tsne-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when you have an imputed single-cell expression matrix and known cell-type labels, and need a 2D embedding (t-SNE after PCA) to visually and quantitatively check cell-type separation.
